- **A07-SP-154** (P08-AP14; STUDY sections 13) — (added in the check) — The trading process reads the strategy files at boot, for the objects that strategy control and screening use, and again at the end of the day, when A12's summary builds A07's direction map from the relative directory `"config"` (P08-S062; A02 F-11). A strategy file changed during the day, or a `--config` directory other than `config`, would give the summary's direction labels a different source from the loaded objects. Is this a point on A07's side, or only on A12's and A02's (F-11), with A07 recording that its function re-reads the files whenever it is called? (Boundary point, reported, not resolved.)
- **A07-SP-155** (P08-AP15; STUDY sections 13) — (added in the check) — Seven of the `v3_chain` gate keys reach A07's playbook gate functions as required keyword arguments with no fallback in A07's code, and three are used only by A18's callers to compute the gates' inputs (P08-S034; P05-S090). The gate functions convert the seven with `float()` and test no range (`screening/hard_gate.py` L271, L289, L411, L414, L449 to L450), and of the seven A02's schema checks only `rr_floor > 0` (`core/config_loader.py` L1504 to L1505; P05-S090). P05-AP22 raises the validation of the `v3_chain` settings on the schema side; does the AUDIT take the gate functions' side as part of P05-AP22, or as a separate point shared with A02 (schema) and A18 (callers)?
- **A07-SP-156** (P09-AP02; STUDY sections 9) — No single authoritative screening record: when a signal has more than one row, the dashboard takes `MAX`, the trade log, the latency reader and the replay take the newest, the trade review keeps the highest score in one map and an order-dependent row (a later `PASSED` row or a later higher score) in its signal table, the forward shadow recorder records every row, and the daily report and the trade journal keep the last row of an unordered result (P09-S035; corrected in the check with P09-S034). Is any rule for repeated rows defined anywhere? (Boundary: the readers are A15's and A19's.)
- **A07-SP-157** (P09-AP04; STUDY sections 10) — No per-signal or per-symbol guard in A07: the screener, the gate and strategy control read no stored status and hold no claim; A06's claim is evicted after 60 s and is not held by a gate resumption (P09-S048; P06-AP10). Is A07 meant to rely on A06's claim alone? (Boundary: A06's claim and sweeper; A09's one-trade check.)
- **A07-SP-158** (P09-AP06; STUDY sections 9, 10) — Store contention and silent outcomes: every A07 write waits up to 30 s for the write lock and a failure is swallowed (screener, gate) or ends a run (scripts), with no signal to the caller (P09-S032, P09-S049, P09-S093). Is the absence of any surfaced failure, other than log lines, within the design (SS5, SS10, EG7)? (Boundary: A03's store.)
- **A07-SP-159** (P09-AP07; STUDY sections 9) — Commit boundaries: the screener commits status before row, the gate's `add` row before status (unreachable), and the gate's release commits both tables after the memory change; none is retried (P09-S021 to P09-S025, P09-S032). How should A07's side of A03 P-11 (5) be recorded, given that the orders differ between components? (Boundary: A03 P-11.)
- **A07-SP-160** (P09-AP09; STUDY sections 9) — The stored reason and time: `signals.rejection_reason` mixes five kinds of value, and no A07 status change has a stored time in `signals` (P09-S016, P09-S040 (g)). Is the record sufficient for the readers' "why" and "when" questions? (Boundary: A03 P-02, A06-12.)
- **A07-SP-161** (P09-AP10; STUDY sections 9) — One word, three meanings: `TIMEOUT` as a step status, as a signal status of an ambiguous placement, and inside `GATE_RELEASED_TIMEOUT` (P09-S040 (h)). Is the shared word a risk for the readers?
- **A07-SP-162** (P09-AP11; STUDY sections 11) — The tier as a sizing input: at the committed files the live total cannot reach `HIGH` and reaches `MEDIUM` only at exactly 65 without timeouts, so a pass reaches A09's sizer as `LOW` unless its total is at least 65 (`MEDIUM`; above 65 only with timed-out steps) (P09-S040 (i), P09-S078; P05-S029; corrected in the check from "almost every pass"). - **A07-SP-163** (P09-AP13; STUDY sections 10) — The 2 % circuit margin is applied before the fill by A07's rule on the trigger price and after the fill by A10's placeability gate on the exit legs; on the shadow path the same rule runs twice per screening (P09-S059, P09-S060). Are the two applications meant to agree for one signal? (Boundary: A09 for the constant, A10 for the placeability gate.)
- **A07-SP-164** (P09-AP14; STUDY sections 11) — Two quotes for one passed signal: the screening decision uses the screener's quote and, for non-pullback strategies, the placement basis uses a later quote fetched after `PASSED` (P09-S064). - **A07-SP-166** (P09-AP16; STUDY sections 9, 11) — `PASSED` as a lasting status: with the allocator in enforce mode the processor returns while the status is still `PASSED`, and A15's classifier counts `PASSED` as "qualified" (P09-S082, P09-S034). ## 19. Draft questions about intent for Rama

Only where the code and the legacy documents cannot answer. These are drafts gathered from the ten parts and merged where they overlap: none has been raised with Rama, each stays OPEN FOR RAMA, and nothing is decided in this STUDY (Document 04, section 4, step 8). Each asks only about how the legacy system was meant to work, or about a records point, never about the new system. Three are marked by their parts for Claude and ChatGPT to decide whether to put them at all (A07-Q06, A07-Q07, A07-Q09), and two only if the AUDIT cannot settle them from the records (A07-Q20, A07-Q21). For each: the question with its options, the evidence that leaves the intent open, the source IDs and the STUDY section.

**Strategy selection and control (section 3)**

- **A07-Q01** (P02-R1; from P02-AP07; section 3). For a DELIVERY strategy blocked both by the breaker (`force_intraday_only` on) and by `trade_type: INTRADAY`, which reject label was meant to appear? A: `TRADE_TYPE` (the trade-type mismatch shown distinctly); B: `STRATEGY_CONTROL` (the breaker first, as the code does); Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the Phase-4 comment states why `TRADE_TYPE` exists, but no source states the intended order when both apply (P02-S012, P02-S019).
- **A07-Q02** (P02-R2; from P02-AP01 and P08-AP06; section 3). When `trade_type` holds a value other than INTRADAY, DELIVERY or BOTH, what was the strategy-control check meant to do? A: let strategies trade as under BOTH (what the resolver does); B: let no strategy trade (fail closed); C: such a value was never expected to reach the check, because the configuration loader refuses it; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: `VALID_TRADE_TYPES` is defined in the resolver but unused; two legacy audits recorded the fall-through, one calling it "mitigated by the config_loader validator"; no source states the intended handling (P02-S011, P02-S020, P02-S121).
- **A07-Q03** (P02-R3 and P07-R1, merged; from P02-AP10 and P07-AP01; sections 3 and 12). History, in two parts. (a) What was "PAPER_ONLY" meant to mean for a strategy in the live system? A: the strategy stops taking live entries (like `enabled: false`); B: the strategy keeps running for records only, with no orders; C: the text was left over from an earlier paper-and-live design; Other / qualify. (b) What was the metrics script's demotion rule (the three latest Sharpe values below -0.3) meant to do? A: stop the strategy's new entries automatically (a state that the trading path reads); B: alert the operator, who then switches the strategy off in its file; C: only record the condition for review; D: the text was left over from an earlier design and no effect was intended; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the docstring says the script "auto-sets strategy to PAPER_ONLY and sends Telegram CRITICAL alert", but no tracked text defines the state or the operator's action, and the code writes nothing and sends nothing on its declared route (P02-S077, P02-S119; P07-S030 to P07-S034).

**The secondary screener (section 4)**

- **A07-Q04** (P03-R1; from P03-AP02; section 4). When one of the ten screening steps other than signal age scores zero (for example volume or VWAP position), what was meant to happen to the signal? A: reject it with that step's name (as module 29's test list, written 17-Apr-2026, expected: "REJECTED_volume_surge"); B: let it lose only that step's points, so that it is rejected only if the total falls below the minimum (what the code does, and what a later test comment expects); C: reject it only for certain steps (please name them); Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the sources differ and none states which was decided (P03-S012, P03-S067, P03-S071; the vocabulary difference P10-AP05).
- **A07-Q05** (P03-R2; from P03-AP11 and P05-AP21; sections 4 and 7). On the V3 enforce path (never the committed setting), if a strategy had a small positive `min_score` that the re-scale turns into 0, what was meant? A: no score floor for that strategy (what the code would do); B: the V3 global minimum (`v3_min_pass_score`) should apply instead; C: such small overrides were never expected (all strategies use 0); Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the re-scale's docstring states its formula and that 0 stays 0, but not the case of a positive value that becomes 0 (P03-S052; `screening/hard_gate.py` L206 to L212).

**The market data and the ten steps (sections 5 and 13)**

- **A07-Q06** (P04-S169; from P04-S140; sections 5 and 13; marked by P04 for Claude and ChatGPT to decide whether to put to Rama). In the strategy files, were `max_spread_pct: 0.005` and `min_adr_pct: 0.005` meant as fractions (0.005 = 0.5 %, as S4 reads if these two fields are among its "Percentage float fields", and as the module instruction "default 0.5%" reads) or as percentages (0.005 = 0.005 %, as the step code computes)? A: fractions (0.5 %); B: percentages (0.005 %); Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the sources conflict, and the legacy record's own question to Rama (OQ-P2-1) is unanswered at the baseline; the STUDY records the conflict either way (P04-S103 to P04-S105, P04-S140).
- **A07-Q07** (P04-S172; sections 5 and 13; marked by P04 for Claude and ChatGPT to decide whether to put to Rama). In step 1 (volume surge), was `avg_volume_20d` meant as the 20-day average of full-day volume, compared with Kite's part-day `volume` ("Volume traded today") at the moment of the quote, or as another measure (for example an average of the volume up to the same time of day)? A: a full-day 20-day average; B: a time-of-day-matched average; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: SE4, SS7 and the weights file name the input only; the input is never produced at the baseline, so the answer changes no recorded legacy behaviour (P04-S058, P04-S061, P04-S070, P04-S071, P04-S167).
- **A07-Q08** (P08-R1; from P08-AP01; sections 5 and 13). The strategy files set `min_volume_surge` differently per strategy (1.2 to 2.0), but the screener never received the 20-day average volume (nor the ATR used with `min_adr_pct`), so these values could not change any result. When the values were set, what was intended? A: they were placeholders, to act once those market-data inputs were wired; B: they were expected to filter signals already; C: not known or not decided; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: no source states the intent for the period without the inputs; the legacy integrity audit later recorded them as having no effect and read the tuning variation as "intent that has never executed" (P08-S074; A07-Q07 asks the related question on step 1's input).

**The score floor (sections 6, 13 and 15)**

- **A07-Q09** (P10-R1; from P10-AP14 and P10-U07; sections 6, 13 and 15; marked by P10 for Claude and ChatGPT to decide whether to put to Rama). History only: the legacy project's decision records list two open decisions on the screening score floor, D3 (whether to keep `min_pass_score` at 60 or change it) and whether to freeze `min_pass_score` while measurements accumulated, and the integrity audit asked whether the change 60 → 55 → 60 in July 2026 was deliberate. Were these decided before the system stopped? A: yes, the floor of 60 was the intended setting; B: yes, another value or rule was intended (please state it); C: never decided; D: other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: both decision records are OPEN in `docs/decisions/`, and OQ-P2-2 is unanswered in the repository (P10-S063, P10-S080; P10-AP14, P10-U07).

**The HardGate and the playbook gates (section 7)**

- **A07-Q10** (P05-R1; from P05-AP03 and P05-U12; section 7). The V3 playbook gates (the S&R reward-to-risk gate, the 1-hour trend gate, the market-extreme gate and the two PB-01 gates) cite a "V3 DECISION CONTENT SPECIFICATION v1.0" that is not in the repository, and their numbers are marked "SEEDS" (reward-to-risk at least 2.0; stop buffer 0.20 × ATR; confirmation body at least 0.50 of the candle; volume at least 1.20 × the baseline; pullback within 0.5 % of the level or 0.5 × ATR; hold buffer 0.20 × ATR). Which describes what was decided? A: these numbers were the intended starting rules, to be calibrated from shadow data; B: other values were decided (please give them, or say where they are recorded); C: the gates were exploratory and no values were decided; Other / qualify. If such a document existed outside the repository, please say where it is kept. Evidence leaving it open: one legacy log says Step 10a was "Ratified via" that specification, while the legacy pending registers say it does not exist (P05-S013, P05-S090, P05-S091, P05-S116).
- **A07-Q11** (P05-R2; from P05-AP05; section 7). For the PB-01 confirmation candle, the code accepts any candle that closes above the level with a large enough body, including one that closes below its own open. What was meant? A: the candle must also close above its open (a rising candle); B: any large body counts (what the code does); C: not decided; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: no source states the candle's direction (P05-S087).
- **A07-Q12** (P05-R3; from P05-AP02; section 7). The V3 hard-gate plan of 11-Jul-2026 listed a liquidity check that rejects a signal when no liquidity data exists; the code made liquidity a no-op, citing "A8", and kept the spread as a scored step. What was decided? A: no liquidity reject (the spread stays a score), as the code does; B: a liquidity reject was intended but not built; C: not known; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the meaning of the label "A8" is not recorded in the repository (P05-S010, P05-S011, P05-S042; P05-U09).

**The entry gate (section 8)**

- **A07-Q13** (P06-R1; from P06-AP01 and P06-AP15; section 8). The six strategies marked `pullback_wait_enabled: true` (first pullback long and short, open-high breakdown short, open-low breakout long, VWAP bounce long, VWAP rejection short) were designed to wait at the entry gate for a pullback, but at the baseline nothing sends any signal to the gate, so they enter directly from the trigger-based prices. Which describes your understanding while the system ran? A: you knew the gate was not connected and accepted the direct entry for these strategies; B: you believed these strategies waited at the gate; C: you were not aware either way; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: no source states a decision; the legacy entry IA-P2-01 of 01-Aug-2026 records the gap and leaves the choice "wire vs ..." to a future decision (P06-S014, P06-S044, P06-S046, P06-S047).
- **A07-Q14** (P06-R2; from P06-U05; section 8). Did you ever restore or copy a database file into the production system, edit the production database by hand, or run tests or drills against the production database file? A: never; B: yes (please say when and what); C: not sure; Other. Evidence leaving it open: this decides whether any `gate_state` row could have come from outside the committed code; no repository source records it (P06-S045).

**Retries and repeated screening (section 10)**

- **A07-Q15** (P09-R1; from P09-AP01 and P03-AP09; section 10). When an order was refused by the broker connection's rate limit and the system re-tried the signal (up to three times), what was meant to happen to the signal's screening? A: it is screened again from the start, with a fresh quote and the signal's age at that moment, as the code does, so a signal that passed the first time can be rejected on the retry; B: the first screening decision is kept and only the order is retried; C: not decided; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the code comment says only that the retry is "idempotent" towards the broker, since nothing was sent; it states nothing about re-screening (P09-S088).

**The strategy scripts (section 12)**

- **A07-Q16** (P07-R2; from P07-AP04; section 12). History: what was the stored strategy "Sharpe" meant to measure? A: the standard Sharpe ratio on returns over a riskless rate, annualised; B: an internal consistency score of daily P&L, with the Sharpe form as a convenience; C: no specific intent beyond the formula in the docstring; Other / qualify. Evidence leaving it open: the docstring says "Recomputed each run" (L112) and a test expects a return to OK "when realized matches", but no source states the window (P07-S060, P07-S062, P07-S075).
